00237: function resolveUfFromLotteryKey(lotteryKey) {
00238:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00239:   if (!lk) return null;
00240:   if (lk === RJ_LOTTERY_KEY) return RJ_STATE_CODE;
00241:   if (lk === FEDERAL_LOTTERY_KEY) return FEDERAL_STATE_CODE;
00242:   if (lk === LOOK_LOTTERY_KEY) return LOOK_STATE_CODE;
00243:   if (lk === NACIONAL_LOTTERY_KEY) return NACIONAL_STATE_CODE;
00244: 
00245:   // se for uma UF padrão de 2 letras (SP, MG, DF etc.), preserva
00246:   if (/^[A-Z]{2}$/.test(lk)) return lk;
00247: 
00248:   // fallback: não inventa UF
00249:   return null;
00250: }

00247: function extractHourFromText(text) {
00248:   const s = String(text ?? "").trim();

00252:   const mIso = s.match(/\b(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::\d{2})?\b/);
00253:   if (mIso) return `${pad2(mIso[1])}:${pad2(mIso[2])}`;

00258:   }
00259:   return "";

OCORRÊNCIA NA LINHA: 21
00013:   const s = String(v ?? "").trim().toUpperCase();
00014: 
00015:   if (["RJ", "RIO", "PT-RIO", "PT_RIO"].includes(s)) return "PT_RIO";
00016: 
00017:   if (["FED", "FEDERAL", "BR"].includes(s)) return "FEDERAL";
00018: 
00019:   if (["LOOK", "GO"].includes(s)) return "LOOK";
00020: 
00021:   if (["NACIONAL", "LT_NACIONAL", "LT-NACIONAL"].includes(s)) {
00022:     return "NACIONAL";
00023:   }
00024: 
00025:   return null;
00026: }

00032: function pad2(n) {
00033:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
00034: }
00035: 
00036: function isISODateStrict(s) {
00037:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
00038:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(str)) return false;

00237: function resolveUfFromLotteryKey(lotteryKey) {
00238:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00239:   if (!lk) return null;
00240:   if (lk === RJ_LOTTERY_KEY) return RJ_STATE_CODE;
00241:   if (lk === FEDERAL_LOTTERY_KEY) return FEDERAL_STATE_CODE;
00242:   if (lk === LOOK_LOTTERY_KEY) return LOOK_STATE_CODE;
00243:   if (lk === NACIONAL_LOTTERY_KEY) return NACIONAL_STATE_CODE;
00244: 
00245:   // se for uma UF padrão de 2 letras (SP, MG, DF etc.), preserva
00246:   if (/^[A-Z]{2}$/.test(lk)) return lk;
00247: 
00248:   // fallback: não inventa UF
00249:   return null;
00250: }

00247: function extractHourFromText(text) {
00248:   const s = String(text ?? "").trim();
00249:   if (!s) return "";
00250:   const mh = s.match(/\b(\d{1,2})\s*(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)\b/i);
00251:   if (mh) return `${pad2(mh[1])}:00`;
00252:   const mIso = s.match(/\b(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::\d{2})?\b/);
00253:   if (mIso) return `${pad2(mIso[1])}:${pad2(mIso[2])}`;
00254:   const mBare = s.match(/(?:^|\D)(\d{1,2})(?:\D|$)/);
00255:   if (mBare) {
00256:     const n = Number(mBare[1]);
00257:     if (Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 0 && n <= 23) return `${pad2(n)}:00`;
00258:   }
00259:   return "";
00260: }

00217: function clampYmd(ymd, minDate, maxDate) {
00218:   const d = normalizeToYMD(ymd);
00219:   if (!isYMD(d)) return null;
00220: 
00221:   let out = d;
00222: 
00223:   if (isYMD(minDate) && out < minDate) out = minDate;
00224:   if (isYMD(maxDate) && out > maxDate) out = maxDate;
00225: 
00226:   return out;
00227: }

00241: function normalizeCloseHourForLottery(lotteryInput, value) {
00242:   const normalized = normalizeHourLike(value);
00243:   if (!normalized) return "";
00244: 
00245:   const lotteryKey = canonicalScopeKey(lotteryInput);
00246: 
00247:   if (lotteryKey !== "NACIONAL") {
00248:     return normalized;
00249:   }
00250: 
00251:   const nacionalHourMap = {
00252:     "01:49": "02:00",
00253:     "07:49": "08:00",
00254:     "09:49": "10:00",
00255:     "11:49": "12:00",
00256:     "14:49": "15:00",
00257:     "16:49": "17:00",
00258:     "20:49": "21:00",
00259:     "22:49": "23:00",
00260:   };
00261: 
00262:   return nacionalHourMap[normalized] || normalized;
00263: }
